# QA fixes and evidence — 2026-10-31

All 133 flags reviewed individually; a flag was not treated as proof that its comparator is correct. **75 foods changed** (including review metadata), **505 field changes including aliases**, **0 food slugs added/removed/renamed**, **0 recipe edits**. Every field of the Greek-yogurt record is unchanged. All work is in the permitted output paths; the backup is read only.

| Class | Meaning | Flags |
|---|---|---:|
| a | Wrong food, misleading identity or cooking proxy corrected | 22 |
| b | Household portion corrected or explicitly justified by size | 6 |
| c | Legitimate analysis / definition / sample difference; retain value | 70 |
| d | Insufficient independent compatible evidence; retain value | 35 |

Counts are **field flags**, not food counts; a food may have both a correction and an unresolved fibre question. Every c decision adds `second_source_differs` plus the other source/value in `origin_note`; every d adds `needs_second_source`. Every changed food, including metadata-only review, has `changed_after_qa` and an old/new/source trail.

## Five priority corrections

| Food | Before → after, per 100 g | Evidence and limits |
|---|---|---|
| labneh | kcal 94.5071 → 151.33; protein 8.7789 → 8.63 g; carbohydrate 4.754 → 6.14 g; fat 4.394 → 10.25 g | [Kaaki et al. 2012 Table 2](https://www.journalofdairyscience.org/article/S0022030212000045/pdf), full-fat assay means. Energy = 4×(8.63+6.14)+9×10.25. NFE used as carbohydrate; optional Greek-yogurt micronutrients cleared. Fibre 0 separately supported by [Khider et al., unsupplemented C2](https://link.springer.com/article/10.1007/s43994-022-00003-8); that paper's inconsistent protein tables were excluded. Medium confidence; Greek yogurt itself unchanged. |
| Fish | Sea bass raw: 97/18.43/0/2 → 168/20/0/9.8; cooked: 124/23.63/0/2.56 → 154/23.2/0/6.8 (kcal/P/C/F). Grilled Atlantic mackerel: 262/23.85/0/17.81 → 283/20.3/0/22.4. Grilled tilapia and fish-grilled: 128/26.15/0/2.65 → 122.64/28.41/0/1.00. | [CoFID 16-385/386/394](https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/composition-of-foods-integrated-dataset-cofid), backed by [DH 2013 assays](https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/nutrient-analysis-of-fish). [Sea-bass sampling](https://assets.publishing.service.gov.uk/media/5a7b609bed915d3ed9063fe2/Nutrient_analysis_of_fish_and_fish_products_-_Sampling_Report.pdf) identifies European sea bass and includes farmed fillets; no claim it is exclusively farmed or wild. Cooked bass still uses a visibly named baked-flesh proxy. Tilapia: [El-Shehawy et al. 2012, Table 1 p.128](https://jfds.journals.ekb.eg/article_75312_4a19344d443d6024db457c326cabaa8a.pdf), wet-weight Nile-tilapia flesh: 69.90% moisture, 0.69% ash, 28.41% protein, 1% oil. Residual carbohydrate = 0; kcal calculated 4/4/9. All medium; one tilapia assay does not define all grilling. |
| Raw 10%-fat ground beef | kcal 190.1348 → 176; fat 12.85 → 10 g; complete profile from one matching record | [USDA SR Legacy 174030](https://fdc.nal.usda.gov/food-details/174030/nutrients) is explicitly raw 90/10. Foundation measured 12.85 g did not match the label precision. No second 12.85%-fat food added: no recipe/plan need. Medium confidence reflects declared-fat reference versus local sample. |
| rumi-cheese | kcal 387 → 324.65; P 31.8 → 22.16; C 3.63 → 1.29; F 26.94 → 25.65 g | [Egypt. J. Food Sci. 51(2), Materials p.258](https://journals.ekb.eg/article_328053_135b62aeabeb84b1c010124cf19c7d3a.pdf): Ras ingredient, not the processed spread. Energy calculated. [Shahin et al. Table 1](https://ejfs.journals.ekb.eg/article_298282_fa9a386870512e4df07cb84850431d3c.pdf) corroborates variable protein/fat with ripening; unlike samples were not averaged. Medium, sample-specific; optional Romano micronutrients removed. Fibre 0 remains a named USDA Romano fallback requiring a second source. |
| whey-protein | 352/78.13/6.25/1.56/3.1 → 399.73/77.07/8.97/5.91/0.7 (kcal/P/C/F/fibre) | Equal-weight reference model from [Myprotein unflavoured label](https://www.myprotein.ae/p/sports-nutrition/myprotein-impact-whey-protein-unflavoured/12207745/), [Bulk published concentrate label](https://www.bulk.com/uk/products/pure-whey-protein/bpb-wpc8-0000) and [WPC-80 assay](https://www.mdpi.com/2072-6643/10/9/1178). Fibre uses only exact label values; assay <0.5 was excluded from averaging. `brand_varies`, concentrate scope and label override explicit; 30 g weighed scoop retained. Medium model confidence, not a market survey. |

Raw Atlantic mackerel remains 205 kcal/13.89 g fat: USDA is species-specific, while UK 233/17.9 establishes seasonal/sample variation rather than an extraction error. Names and aliases now say Atlantic. Other seafood labels identify the source species, explicitly unspecified/mixed species, or the actual proxy. `fish-grilled` is explicitly Nile tilapia in both languages and all aliases. Sea-bream remains a low-confidence **named mixed-sea-bass proxy**; no honest species-specific replacement was established. No new species food was needed.

The tilapia publisher PDF returned 429; the primary paper was read through its [author repository full text](https://www.researchgate.net/publication/298802825_NUTRITIONAL_VALUE_AND_FATTY_ACIDS_COMPOSITION_IN_THE_MOST_COMMON_EGYPTIAN_FISH_AS_AFFECTED_BY_TRADITIONAL_GRILLING_PROCESS). Only wet-weight columns were used; burgers, grey mullet and calculated Brazilian raw-derived profiles were rejected as replacements. The assay describes a 20-minute hot-plate method, coating removed when flesh was filleted. Tilapia fibre 0 alone retains the explicit USDA fallback; optional US micronutrients were cleared.

## Whey calculation inputs

| Source | kcal | Protein g | Carbohydrate g | Fat g | Fibre g |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| [source](https://www.myprotein.ae/p/sports-nutrition/myprotein-impact-whey-protein-unflavoured/12207745/) | 412.00000 | 82 | 4 | 7.5 | 0 |
| [source](https://www.bulk.com/uk/products/pure-whey-protein/bpb-wpc8-0000) | 378.00000 | 71 | 15 | 3.5 | 1.4 |
| [source](https://www.mdpi.com/2072-6643/10/9/1178) | 409.17782 | 78.2 | 7.9 | 6.72 | <0.5 (excluded) |

Macro/energy outputs are arithmetic means of all three rows rounded to two decimals. Fibre = (0+1.4)/2 = 0.7; it is a two-label estimate with a different evidence set, explicitly not an assayed blend. WPC energy = 1712/4.184. This is an illustrative concentrate profile; isolates and gainers need separate real labels. Optional nutrients without a common verified basis stay null.

| 45 | bread-baladi | protein_g_per_100g | 9.8 / 7.595680000000001 | c | An actual Egyptian baladi control was used, not UK sliced bread. The paper reports protein 11.60, fat 1.62, crude fibre 1.65 and carbohydrate 83.63 on dry matter, with moisture 34.52%. Multiply by 0.6548. Our comparator carbohydrate adds crude fibre back to approximate total carbohydrate. Crude fibre is NOT total dietary fibre; the seed 6.1 g fibre remains independently unresolved. Calculated energy is an estimate, not published calorimetry. [source](https://www.nature.com/articles/s41598-023-48686-1) |

## Every changed field — actual diff against the immutable backup

Arrays (portions, aliases and review flags) are shown as complete field values, dictionaries are diffed by leaf, and origin-note history is retained in full. No field is omitted because it is metadata. Numeric nutrition entries are per 100 g; portion weights are grams. Raw machine-readable diff: `out/f-scripts/field-changes.json`.

QA review: retained calories_kcal_per_100g=90.0; other source reports 109; SR corroborates the low Foundation values, but an additional independent UK drained-brine sample has 109 kcal and 24.9 g protein. Neither 90 nor 116 kcal is universally correct: drainage, species and formulation must be specified. The assumed 140 g can requires a drained-weight label. QA review: retained protein_g_per_100g=9.8; other source reports 7.595680000000001; An actual Egyptian baladi control was used, not UK sliced bread. The paper reports protein 11.60, fat 1.62, crude fibre 1.65 and carbohydrate 83.63 on dry matter, with moisture 34.52%. Multiply by 0.6548. Our comparator carbohydrate adds crude fibre back to approximate total carbohydrate. Crude fibre is NOT total dietary fibre; the seed 6.1 g fibre remains independently unresolved. Calculated energy is an estimate, not published calorimetry. Broad beans with olive oil; bread is not included. Oil quantity varies materially. Lebanese preparation is a proxy for Egyptian restaurant food; no ingredient reconstruction. Preprint fibre measurements need independent confirmation. Serving weight is an explicit collector assumption unless a portion basis states otherwise. ## All 90 screened foods: retained values and reasons

“Retained” below refers to nutrition, even when review metadata, identity or portions changed. Each food has one line; the field ledger above separates mixed decisions.

| Food | Outcome / reason |
|---|---|
| chicken-breast-raw | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| chicken-breast-grilled | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| chicken-breast-boiled | Nutrition retained. CoFID meat-only casseroled chicken is a moist-heat proxy, not plain boiled-and-drained breast. Fat can reflect skin exposure and cooking losses. Confirm the actual cooking protocol before changing numbers. |
| chicken-thigh-raw | Nutrition retained. Same skinless raw cut, but Foundation fat 7.916 g is 92% above SR 4.12 g. Analytical trimming/sample differences are plausible; no copy error was found. Specify trimming and avoid representing this as a precise universal value. |
| chicken-thigh-grilled | Nutrition retained. CoFID roasted dark meat is broader than thigh alone, while the seed already uses roasted thigh for grilled. Fat differences are a proxy/cut sensitivity, not proof of a decimal error. |
| chicken-thigh-boiled | Nutrition retained. CoFID NSP is explicitly 0; AOAC total dietary fibre is unavailable. Zero-NSP screen only, not an independently reported TDF value. |
| beef-lean-raw | Nutrition retained. CoFID NSP is explicitly 0; AOAC total dietary fibre is unavailable. Zero-NSP screen only, not an independently reported TDF value. |
| beef-lean-cooked | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| beef-ground-10-fat-raw | Nutrition corrected: calories_kcal_per_100g, protein_g_per_100g, fat_g_per_100g. Replacement evidence and limitations in changed-field table. |
| beef-ground-10-fat-cooked | Nutrition retained. UK generic stewed mince has unspecified starting fat and a different cooking method from the seed broiled patty. This is a weak comparator; its protein discrepancy cannot establish that the seed is wrong. |
| beef-ground-20-fat-raw | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| beef-ground-20-fat-cooked | Nutrition retained. CoFID NSP is explicitly 0; AOAC total dietary fibre is unavailable. Zero-NSP screen only, not an independently reported TDF value. |
| beef-liver-raw | Nutrition retained. Both sources say raw bovine liver, but UK fat is 7.8 versus 3.63 g and UK carbohydrate is trace. These are historical sample and analytical-definition differences. Do not copy the UK zero/trace carbohydrate over the seed automatically. CoFID NSP is explicitly 0; AOAC total dietary fibre is unavailable. Zero-NSP screen only, not an independently reported TDF value. |
| liver-beef-cooked | Nutrition retained. UK stewed versus US braised liver differ materially in fat and protein. Cooking losses and residual cooking fat need a stated protocol. |
| tilapia-raw | Nutrition retained. Seed fibre already comes from this same SR fallback. The fibre comparison is circular and does not count as independent. Identity clarified. |
| tilapia-grilled | Nutrition corrected: calories_kcal_per_100g, protein_g_per_100g, fat_g_per_100g. Replacement evidence and limitations in changed-field table. Portion size/wording corrected or alternatives added. Identity clarified. |
| sea-bass-raw | Nutrition corrected: calories_kcal_per_100g, protein_g_per_100g, fat_g_per_100g. Replacement evidence and limitations in changed-field table. Portion size/wording corrected or alternatives added. Identity clarified. |
| sea-bass-grilled | Nutrition corrected: calories_kcal_per_100g, protein_g_per_100g, fat_g_per_100g. Replacement evidence and limitations in changed-field table. Portion size/wording corrected or alternatives added. Identity clarified. |
| mullet-raw | Nutrition retained. CoFID NSP is explicitly 0; AOAC total dietary fibre is unavailable. Zero-NSP screen only, not an independently reported TDF value. Identity clarified. |
| mackerel-raw | Nutrition retained. Fat varies with catch, season and species. UK measured samples are fattier. Confirm the mackerel species and season; this discrepancy alone does not establish an extraction mistake. Identity clarified. |
| mackerel-grilled | Nutrition corrected: calories_kcal_per_100g, protein_g_per_100g, fat_g_per_100g. Replacement evidence and limitations in changed-field table. Identity clarified. |
| tuna-canned-water | Nutrition retained. SR corroborates the low Foundation values, but an additional independent UK drained-brine sample has 109 kcal and 24.9 g protein. Neither 90 nor 116 kcal is universally correct: drainage, species and formulation must be specified. The assumed 140 g can requires a drained-weight label. Seed fibre already comes from this same SR fallback. The fibre comparison is circular and does not count as independent. Identity clarified. |
| tuna-canned-oil | Nutrition retained. Both are drained, but UK explicitly uses sunflower oil and the US record covers oil generically. Remaining oil and drainage change fat and calories; record drained mass and oil type. Identity clarified. |
| shrimp-cooked | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. Identity clarified. |
| fish-grilled | Nutrition corrected: calories_kcal_per_100g, protein_g_per_100g, fat_g_per_100g. Replacement evidence and limitations in changed-field table. Identity clarified. |
| egg-raw | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| egg-boiled | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| egg-white | Nutrition retained. Foundation carbohydrate 2.36 g versus SR 0.73 g crosses the absolute threshold by only 0.13 g (difference 1.63 g). Likely analytical/by-difference variation, not a demonstrated nutrient-ID error. |
| milk-full-fat | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| milk-low-fat | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| milk-skimmed | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| yogurt-plain | Nutrition retained. Both whole-milk plain yogurts, but Foundation has 4.484 g fat versus SR 3.25 g, giving 77.3 versus 61 kcal. The higher-fat sample is real; define a fat range rather than assuming all plain yogurt is interchangeable. |
| yogurt-greek | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| labneh | Nutrition corrected: calories_kcal_per_100g, protein_g_per_100g, carbs_g_per_100g, fat_g_per_100g. Replacement evidence and limitations in changed-field table. |
| domiati-cheese | Nutrition retained. The seed is a feta proxy, not independently measured Domiati. UK feta is a second check of the proxy only; differences in protein/carbs do not identify the correct Egyptian white cheese. Source a matching Domiati analysis, including milk and ripening. Independent numbers only verify a feta proxy, not Domiati. Identity clarified. |
| feta-cheese | Nutrition retained. Foundation crumbled whole-milk feta has 19.71 g protein versus SR 14.21 g, and 5.58 versus 3.88 g carbohydrate. This is a same-food discrepancy, plausibly moisture/formulation; verify against the intended local white cheese. |
| rumi-cheese | Nutrition corrected: calories_kcal_per_100g, protein_g_per_100g, carbs_g_per_100g, fat_g_per_100g. Replacement evidence and limitations in changed-field table. Identity clarified. |
| cottage-cheese | Nutrition retained. Agreement between US cottage-cheese datasets does not verify Egyptian kareish. Identity clarified. |
| rice-white-raw | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| rice-white-cooked | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| rice-brown-dry | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| rice-brown-cooked | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| pasta-dry | Nutrition retained. No material discrepancy in the chosen numerical screen. Food, sample and household serving sizes still vary; this is not a local portion measurement. The independent comparator does not report total dietary fibre. Unknown was not converted to zero. |
| pasta-cooked | Nutrition retained. UK boiled spaghetti is wetter than US cooked enriched pasta. Hydration/end-point causes material kcal variation; define drained cooking state. Neither raw/dry pasta value has been substituted here. |
| oats-dry | Nutrition retained. UK oat samples have 10.9 g protein, 8.1 g fat and 7.8 g AOAC fibre; seed has 13.15/6.52/10.1. Product/cultivar and method differences merit review, not automatic replacement. Portion size/wording corrected or alternatives added. |
| oats-cooked | Nutrition retained. Both are cooked in water, but UK porridge is more diluted (47 versus 71 kcal). A cooked-oats record needs dry weight and final yield or a standardized recipe. |
| bread-baladi | Nutrition retained. An actual Egyptian baladi control was used, not UK sliced bread. The paper reports protein 11.60, fat 1.62, crude fibre 1.65 and carbohydrate 83.63 on dry matter, with moisture 34.52%. Multiply by 0.6548. Our comparator carbohydrate adds crude fibre back to approximate total carbohydrate. Crude fibre is NOT total dietary fibre; the seed 6.1 g fibre remains independently unresolved. Calculated energy is an estimate, not published calorimetry. The independent comparator does not report total dietary fibre. Unknown was not converted to zero. Portion size/wording corrected or alternatives added. |
| bread-toast-white | Nutrition retained. Both records are untoasted sliced white bread, not dry toast/rusk. UK formulation has less fat/protein and 219 versus 270 kcal. The seed English description makes its state clear, but the Arabic toast name needs that clarification. Slice thickness must also be explicit. Portion size/wording corrected or alternatives added. Identity clarified. |
| bread-toast-whole-wheat | Nutrition retained. Both are untoasted whole-wheat/wholemeal bread, not dried toast. UK protein is lower and kcal differ; flour recipe and moisture vary. |
| potato-boiled | Nutrition retained. UK boiled peeled potato is 74 versus 86 kcal; water content and variety plausibly explain this. Weigh cooked edible potatoes and specify draining. |
| potato-baked | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| sweet-potato-baked | Nutrition retained. Same broad cooking state; UK values are more concentrated in carbohydrate/fibre. Variety and moisture vary materially; avoid one precise number for all Egyptian sweet potatoes. |
| sweet-potato-boiled | Nutrition retained. Both plain boiled flesh, but UK is wetter/lower in carbs. Verify cooked-and-drained yield and variety. |
| fava-beans-dry | Nutrition retained. UK carbohydrate is available carbohydrate in monosaccharide equivalents; USDA carbohydrate-by-difference includes fibre. UK NSP alone is 27.6 g, while total AOAC fibre is unreported. Much of this apparent carbohydrate/energy discrepancy is definitional. Do not replace the seed with 32.5 g total carbohydrate. The independent comparator does not report total dietary fibre. Unknown was not converted to zero. |
| ful-medames | Nutrition retained. Only UK old-food canned reheated/drained broad beans were available as a second screen. Maturity is unspecified and may represent green beans rather than dried mature fava; this is NOT an exact ful match. The 87 kcal/8.3 g protein/12.7 g available carbs/0.7 g fat screen cannot validate or correct Egyptian ful. AOAC fibre absent; NSP 5.2 g is not substituted. UK canned broad-bean maturity is unspecified; no independent exact mature cooked fava/ful match. The independent comparator does not report total dietary fibre. Unknown was not converted to zero. |
| ful-with-oil | Nutrition retained. The Lebanese University report and the cited Hoteit paper reproduce the SAME dish numbers; they are not an independent cross-check. The second comparison is explicitly a 95:5 cooked-bean/oil scenario (148.7 kcal), not a measured Egyptian dish. Extra water or vegetables can explain the lower 116 kcal. Independent recipe-level verification is still missing; standardize beans, oil and final cooked mass. Only an independent ingredient-model sensitivity check; no independent recipe-level analysis. |
| lentils-brown-dry | Nutrition retained. Seed fibre already comes from this same SR fallback. The fibre comparison is circular and does not count as independent. |
| lentils-cooked | Nutrition retained. Both mature lentils boiled, but cooking hydration and UK available-carbohydrate versus USDA total-carbohydrate definitions contribute to the discrepancy. |
| chickpeas-dry | Nutrition retained. Seed fibre already comes from this same SR fallback. The fibre comparison is circular and does not count as independent. |
| chickpeas-cooked | Nutrition retained. Both mature chickpeas boiled, but hydration, fibre assay and carbohydrate definitions differ. The UK fibre comparison is independently sampled in 2017, not simply a republished USDA row. |
| tomato | Nutrition retained. No material discrepancy in the chosen numerical screen. Food, sample and household serving sizes still vary; this is not a local portion measurement. |
| cucumber | Nutrition retained. Most of the carbohydrate difference is UK available carbohydrate versus USDA total carbohydrate including fibre, plus sample variation. A small relative percentage in a low-carb vegetable should not be treated as a decimal error. |
| lettuce | Nutrition retained. UK average lettuce and US green-leaf lettuce are not identical cultivar mixtures. The household cup packing discrepancy is more actionable than the small absolute kcal difference. Portion size/wording corrected or alternatives added. |
| carrot | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| onion-raw | Nutrition retained. Both raw onions; variety and UK available-carb basis explain plausible small kcal variation. |
| broccoli-cooked | Nutrition retained. Both boiled, but UK available carbohydrate omits fibre while US total includes it. Do not replace 7.18 g total carbs with 2.8 g available carbs without a basis conversion. |
| zucchini-cooked | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| spinach-cooked | Nutrition retained. UK carbohydrate excludes fibre; the large percentage is mainly a nutrient-definition issue. UK total AOAC fibre is missing; Canadian boiled-spinach fibre is used as a supplementary check. |
| bell-pepper-green-raw | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| eggplant-raw | Nutrition retained. No material discrepancy in the chosen numerical screen. Food, sample and household serving sizes still vary; this is not a local portion measurement. |
| apple | Nutrition retained. UK available-carb and fibre methods/samples differ from US total carbohydrate. This is a basis/sample sensitivity rather than proven food mismatch. |
| banana | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| orange | Nutrition retained. UK available carbohydrate and older fruit samples are lower; cultivar and edible-part definitions also matter. No evidence of a slipped decimal was found. |
| strawberries | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| watermelon | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| mango | Nutrition retained. UK flesh-only ripe mango is lower in carbohydrate/energy. Ripeness, variety, and carbohydrate definition affect composition. A separate Canadian half-mango reference also shows the seed single-fruit default is a large mango, not a safe universal household mass. Portion size/wording corrected or alternatives added. |
| guava-raw | Nutrition retained. UK guava is flesh-only, whereas USDA common raw guava includes edible skin/seeds. This is an edible-part mismatch; UK 26 kcal must NOT replace 68 kcal for whole edible guava. No compatible independent whole-edible guava AOAC fibre was found. The independent comparator does not report total dietary fibre. Unknown was not converted to zero. Identity clarified. |
| grapes-raw | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| cantaloupe-raw | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| pomegranate-raw | Nutrition retained. Edible arils/seed treatment, analytical sample, and UK carbohydrate definitions differ. Canadian supplementary fibre is much lower too, showing that edible-part/fibre method needs explicit resolution. |
| dates | Nutrition retained. UK generic dried dates have different moisture/cultivars and available-carbohydrate basis. Verify date type and drying stage. |
| dates-medjool | Nutrition retained. UK generic dried dates are not a cultivar-specific Medjool validation. Keep Medjool distinct from small Deglet Noor dates and verify the actual pitted fruit mass. Comparator is generic dried dates, not independently sourced Medjool. |
| olive-oil | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| ghee | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| butter-salted | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| tahini | Nutrition retained. Foundation creamy sesame butter is fattier and lower in carbohydrate than SR roasted/toasted tahini. Hulled/unhulled kernels, roasting and separation affect composition; define the intended tahini type. |
| peanut-butter | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| almonds | Nutrition retained: no flag in the bounded independent screen; this does not verify local equivalence or all household measures. |
| walnuts | Nutrition retained. Foundation fat is higher and carbohydrate lower than SR English walnuts; same broad food, plausible sample and by-difference variability. Retain source precision internally but do not imply six-decimal dietary accuracy. |
| whey-protein | Nutrition corrected: calories_kcal_per_100g, protein_g_per_100g, carbs_g_per_100g, fat_g_per_100g, fiber_g_per_100g. Replacement evidence and limitations in changed-field table. Identity clarified. |

## Recipe impact and plausibility

All 51 recipes were recalculated by `validate-recipes.cjs out`; no failure or implausible revised result required editing recipe quantities. Recipe files remain byte-for-byte equal to backup. Portions expressed as ingredient grams do not automatically change when a food household default changes. These are the recipes whose **calculated nutrition** changed:

| Recipe | Old → new kcal /100 g | New default portion kcal |
|---|---:|---:|
| macarona-bechamel-homemade | 161.90 → 159.54 | 638.2 |
| gym-protein-oats-banana | 119.53 → 123.10 | 492.4 |
| gym-protein-oat-pancakes | 227.07 → 231.04 | 415.9 |
| gym-whey-milk-banana-shake | 81.61 → 85.37 | 324.4 |
| gym-homemade-mass-gainer-shake | 125.97 → 128.21 | 820.5 |
| gym-homemade-protein-bars | 333.81 → 341.49 | 239.0 |

These resulting meal and bar/shake values fall within the validator's energy/yield limits and are plausible for their stated ingredient weights. Local kareish proxy recipes remain uncertain because the food remains a visibly unverified cottage-cheese proxy; no invented nutrient adjustment was made.

## Open questions for a human

- Obtain matching Egyptian Domiati and kareish analyses; those names now explicitly disclose feta/cottage proxies at low confidence. Sea-bream remains a visibly named low-confidence mixed-sea-bass proxy.
- Resolve all 35 d decisions in the flag ledger: independent total dietary fibre (including four circular SR checks), exact mature-fava/ful preparation, oil-to-bean ratio and final yield, cultivar-specific Medjool evidence, and pomegranate edible-aril/seed/fibre definition.
- Check real local labels and ripening stages for Ras/rumi and labneh; the new profiles are medium-confidence source samples, not universal cheese values. Ras zero fibre is a disclosed fallback, not measured zero.
- Confirm farm/wild origin of the actual sea bass and season/species for mackerel. The UK retail sea-bass sample includes farmed fish; it does not isolate wild/farmed composition. Check the client's actual grilling method against the single Egyptian tilapia assay.
- Weigh actual cups, fruit flesh, bread, spoon packing and drained tuna-can mass. The 90 g baladi default applies to the cited subsidized loaf; other bakery loaves need weighing. The 27.3 g toast slice is intentionally retained as a labelled small/thin reference.
- Use the actual whey label by flavour and batch; the equal-weight concentrate model is not an Egyptian market survey, and its fibre estimate uses only two labels.
- No NNI tables, Kaggle data or Open Food Facts bulk data were copied. USDA is CC0; CoFID is OGL (attribution retained); only narrowly selected academic assay facts and expressly requested manufacturer label facts were used, with calculations identified. No permission to reproduce complete commercial tables is asserted.

## Backup and completeness proof

The independent verifier recomputed all 505 changed fields directly from `_backup_before_qa_fixes/` and matched old/new values to the cited ledger. It verified every original food slug, every alias, unchanged Greek yogurt, unchanged 30 g scoop, unchanged recipe bytes, and SHA-256 of every backup file. Hash manifest: `out/f-scripts/fix-summary.json`.
